FKTEN - NUR FARAHIYAH BINTI MOHAMMAD
FKTEN - NUR HIDAYAH BINTI RAMLI
FKTEN - NUR SYAKIMAH BT ISMAIL
FKTEN - NURJULIANA BINTI JUHARI
FKTEN - NUROL HUSNA BINTI CHE ROSE
FKTEN - NURUL FADZLIN BINTI GHAZALI
FKTEN - NURUL IZZA BINTI MOHD NOR
FKTEN - NURULBARIAH BINTI IDRIS

FKTEN - ONG BI LYNN
FKTEN - ONG SIOK LAN
FKTEN - OUNG QI WEI
FKTEN - PRABAKARAN A/L POOPALAN
FKTEN - RASHIDAH BINTI CHE YOB
FKTEN - RAZAIDI BIN HUSSIN
FKTEN - RIZALAFANDE BIN CHE ISMAIL
FKTEN - ROHANI BINTI S MOHAMED FAROOK
FKTEN - ROSLIN BIN JAMALUDIN
FKTEN - ROZANA AINA MAULAT OSMAN
FKTEN - SABRI BIN ZAKARIA
FKTEN - SAFIZAN BINTI SHAARI
FKTEN - SAIDATUL ARDEENAWATIE BINTI AWANG
FKTEN - SANNA BINTI TAKING
FKTEN - SAUFIAH BINTI ABDUL RAHIM
FKTEN - SHAHRIR RIZAL BIN KASJOO

INEE - FOO KAI LOONG
INEE - JASNI BIN MOHAMED ISMAIL
INEE - LIU WEI WEN

INEE - UDA BIN HASHIM
INEE - VOON CHUN HONG
Single gap:
FKTEN - SUZANNA BINTI HARUN RONALD
Border gap:
FKTEN - MOHD SHAIFUL AZIZ BIN RASHID ALI
Big gap:
FKTEN - AHMAD NASIR BIN CHE ROSLI
FKTEN - ZULKIFLI BIN HUSIN
Must come earlier:
FKTEN - ISZAIDY BIN ISMAIL
FKTEN - MOHAMAD SUHAIZAL BIN ABU HASSAN
Must come much earlier:
FKTEN - FAZRUL FAIZ BIN ZAKARIA
FKTEN - MOHD FIKRI BIN CHE HUSIN
FKTEN - MOHD WAFI BIN NASRUDIN
Free day:
FKTEN - ABDUL HAFIIZH BIN ISMAIL

FKTEN - SOHIFUL ANUAR BIN ZAINOL MURAD
FKTEN - STEVEN A/L TANISELASS
FKTEN - SURIATI BINTI SUHAIMI
FKTEN - SYARIFAH NORFAEZAH BT SABKI
FKTEN - SYED MUHAMMAD MAMDUH BIN SYED ZAKARIA
FKTEN - THENNARASAN A/L SABAPATHY
FKTEN - TIJJANI ADAM
FKTEN - VIJAYASARVESWARI A/P VEERAPERUMAL
FKTEN - VIKNESWARAN A/L VIJEAN
FKTEN - WAN AZLIANAWATI BT WAN AZIZ
FKTEN - WAN MOKHDZANI BIN WAN NOR HAIMI

INEE - FOO KAI LOONG
INEE - JASNI BIN MOHAMED ISMAIL
INEE - LIU WEI WEN
INEE - MOHAMAD FARIS BIN MOHAMAD FATHIL
INEE - MOHAMMAD ISA BIN AHMAD AZAN
INEE - MOHAMMAD NUZAIHAN BIN MD NOR
INEE - NOR AZIZAH BINTI PARMIN
INEE - NUR HAMIDAH BINTI ABDUL HALIM
INEE - RAMZAN BIN MAT AYUB
INEE - RUSLINDA BINTI A. RAHIM
INEE - UDA BIN HASHIM
INEE - VOON CHUN HONG
Tuesday
Must come earlier:
FKTEN - FAZRUL FAIZ BIN ZAKARIA
FKTEN - HASNAH BINTI AHMAD
FKTEN - KHAIRUL AFFENDI BIN ROSLI
FKTEN - MOHD FIKRI BIN CHE HUSIN
FKTEN - MOHD WAFI BIN NASRUDIN
Must come much earlier:
FKTEN - AHMAD NASIR BIN CHE ROSLI
FKTEN - MOHAMAD SUHAIZAL BIN ABU HASSAN
FKTEN - MOHD SHAIFUL AZIZ BIN RASHID ALI
Free day:
FKTEN - ABDUL HAFIIZH BIN ISMAIL
FKTEN - AFZAN BINTI KAMARUDIN
FKTEN - AHMAD ASHRAF BIN ABDUL HALIM
FKTEN - AHMAD FARIZ BIN HASAN
FKTEN - AHMAD HUSNI BIN MOHD SHAPRI
FKTEN - AHMAD NASRUL BIN NORALI
FKTEN - AHMAD SYAHIR BIN AHMAD BAKHIT
FKTEN - 'AINI SYUHADA BINTI MD ZAIN
FKTEN - AMIR NAZREN BIN ABDUL RAHIM
FKTEN - ANAS BIN MOHD NOOR
FKTEN - ANISHAZIELA BINTI AZIZAN
FKTEN - ARIF MAWARDI BIN ISMAIL @ SUHAIMEE
FKTEN - ASRAL BIN BAHARI JAMBEK
FKTEN - ASYRAF HAKIMI BIN ABU BAKAR
FKTEN - AZIAN AZAMIMI BINTI ABDULLAH
FKTEN - AZIZI BIN HARUN
FKTEN - AZNOR HANAH BINTI ABDUL HALIM
FKTEN - BANU A/P POOBALAN
FKTEN - BIBI NADIA BINTI TAIB
FKTEN - CHE MUHAMMAD NOR BIN CHE ISA
FKTEN - CHE WAN SHARIFAH ROBIAH BINTI MOHAMAD
FKTEN - CHENG EE MENG
FKTEN - EMI IZHANIZAM BIN AZMI
FKTEN - FAIRUL AFZAL BIN AHMAD FUAD
FKTEN - FAIZAH BINTI ABU BAKAR
FKTEN - FARADILLA BINTI AZIZ
FKTEN - FARIDAH BINTI HASSAN
FKTEN - HAMZAH BIN SAKERAN
FKTEN - HANIZA BINTI YAZID
FKTEN - HARIYANTI BINTI MOHD SALEH
FKTEN - HASLIZA BINTI A RAHIM @ SAMSUDDIN
FKTEN - HASNIZAH BT ARIS
FKTEN - HUSNA BINTI JAMAL ABDUL NASIR
FKTEN - ILI SALWANI BINTI MOHAMAD
FKTEN - ISMAHAYATI BINTI ADAM
FKTEN - KHAIRUL NAJMY BIN ABDUL RANI
FKTEN - LEE HOI LEONG
FKTEN - LIM CHEE CHIN
FKTEN - LIYANA BINTI ZAHID
FKTEN - MAZLEE BIN MAZALAN
FKTEN - MD MAMUN BIN IBNE REAZ
FKTEN - MOHAMAD HALIM BIN ABD. WAHID
FKTEN - MOHAMAD RIZAL BIN ABDUL REJAB
FKTEN - MOHAMED ELSHAIKH ELOBAID SAID AHMED
FKTEN - MOHAMED NASRUN BIN OSMAN

FKTEN - MOHD KHAIRUDDIN BIN MD ARSHAD

FKTEN - ABDUL HAFIIZH BIN ISMAIL
FKTEN - AFZAN BINTI KAMARUDIN
FKTEN - AHMAD ASHRAF BIN ABDUL HALIM
FKTEN - AHMAD FARIZ BIN HASAN
FKTEN - AHMAD HUSNI BIN MOHD SHAPRI
FKTEN - AHMAD NASRUL BIN NORALI
FKTEN - AHMAD SYAHIR BIN AHMAD BAKHIT
FKTEN - 'AINI SYUHADA BINTI MD ZAIN
FKTEN - AMIR NAZREN BIN ABDUL RAHIM
FKTEN - ANAS BIN MOHD NOOR
FKTEN - ANISHAZIELA BINTI AZIZAN
FKTEN - ARIF MAWARDI BIN ISMAIL @ SUHAIMEE
FKTEN - ASRAL BIN BAHARI JAMBEK
FKTEN - ASYRAF HAKIMI BIN ABU BAKAR
FKTEN - AZIAN AZAMIMI BINTI ABDULLAH
FKTEN - AZIZI BIN HARUN
FKTEN - AZNOR HANAH BINTI ABDUL HALIM
FKTEN - BANU A/P POOBALAN
FKTEN - BIBI NADIA BINTI TAIB
FKTEN - CHE MUHAMMAD NOR BIN CHE ISA
FKTEN - CHE WAN SHARIFAH ROBIAH BINTI MOHAMAD
FKTEN - CHENG EE MENG
FKTEN - EMI IZHANIZAM BIN AZMI
FKTEN - FAIRUL AFZAL BIN AHMAD FUAD
FKTEN - FAIZAH BINTI ABU BAKAR
FKTEN - FARADILLA BINTI AZIZ
FKTEN - FARIDAH BINTI HASSAN
FKTEN - HAMZAH BIN SAKERAN
FKTEN - HANIZA BINTI YAZID
FKTEN - HARIYANTI BINTI MOHD SALEH
FKTEN - HASLIZA BINTI A RAHIM @ SAMSUDDIN
FKTEN - HASNIZAH BT ARIS
FKTEN - HUSNA BINTI JAMAL ABDUL NASIR
FKTEN - ILI SALWANI BINTI MOHAMAD
FKTEN - ISMAHAYATI BINTI ADAM
FKTEN - KHAIRUL NAJMY BIN ABDUL RANI
FKTEN - LEE HOI LEONG
FKTEN - LIM CHEE CHIN
FKTEN - LIYANA BINTI ZAHID
FKTEN - MAZLEE BIN MAZALAN
FKTEN - MD MAMUN BIN IBNE REAZ
FKTEN - MOHAMAD HALIM BIN ABD. WAHID
FKTEN - MOHAMAD RIZAL BIN ABDUL REJAB
FKTEN - MOHAMED ELSHAIKH ELOBAID SAID AHMED
FKTEN - MOHAMED NASRUN BIN OSMAN
FKTEN - MOHAMMAD SHAHRIL BIN SALIM
FKTEN - MOHD AMINUDIN BIN JAMLOS
FKTEN - MOHD FAIRUS BIN AHMAD
FKTEN - MOHD FAIRUZ BIN MUHAMAD FADZIL
FKTEN - MOHD FHAIZAL BIN RADZI
FKTEN - MOHD HAFIZ BIN ISMAIL
FKTEN - MOHD HAFIZI BIN OMAR
FKTEN - MOHD HAFIZUDDIN BIN MAT
FKTEN - MOHD HANAFI BIN MAT SOM
FKTEN - MOHD KHAIRUDDIN BIN MD ARSHAD
FKTEN - MOHD KHAIRUL BIN MD KAMIL
FKTEN - MOHD NAJIB BIN MOHD YASIN
FKTEN - MOHD NATASHAH BIN NORIZAN
FKTEN - MOHD NAZRI BIN A. KARIM
FKTEN - MOHD NAZRIN BIN MD ISA
FKTEN - MOHD NORHAFIZ BIN HASHIM
FKTEN - MOHD ROSYDI BIN ZAKARIA
FKTEN - MOHD SALLEHUDIN BIN SAAD
FKTEN - MOHD SYAHRIL BIN NOOR SHAH
FKTEN - MOHD YUSOFF BIN MASHOR
FKTEN - MOHD ZAMRI BIN ZAHIR AHMAD
FKTEN - MOHD. AZARULSANI BIN MD. AZIDIN
FKTEN - MUAMMAR BIN MOHAMAD ISA
FKTEN - MUHAMAD ASMI BIN ROMLI
FKTEN - MUHAMAD HAFIZ BIN AB AZIZ
FKTEN - MUHAMAD SANI BIN MUSTAFA
FKTEN - MUHAMMAD MAHYIDDIN BIN RAMLI
FKTEN - MUHAMMAD SOLIHIN BIN ZULKEFLI
FKTEN - MUHD NUR RAHMAN BIN YAHYA
FKTEN - MUZAMMIL BIN JUSOH
FKTEN - NAIMAH BINTI YAAKOB
FKTEN - NASHRUL FAZLI BIN MOHD NASIR
FKTEN - NAZAHAH BINTI MUSTAFA
FKTEN - NAZUHUSNA BINTI KHALID
FKTEN - NOOR ALIA BINTI MD ZAIN
FKTEN - NOOR SYAZANA BINTI ARSHAD
FKTEN - NOR AZURA MALINI BINTI AHMAD HAMBALI
FKTEN - NOR FARHANI BINTI ZAKARIA
FKTEN - NOR HIDAYAH BINTI SAAD
FKTEN - NOR ROSHIDAH BINTI YUSOF
FKTEN - NORAINI BINTI OTHMAN

FKTEN - NORFATIHAH BINTI BAHARI
FKTEN - NORFAZLIN BINTI RASHID

FKTEN - NORIZAN BINTI MOHAMED NAWAWI
FKTEN - NORLIDA BT ABU BAKAR
FKTEN - NORMAHIRA BINTI MAMAT @ MOHAMAD NOR
FKTEN - NORMALIZA BINTI OMAR
FKTEN - NORSHAMSURI BIN ALI @ HASIM
FKTEN - NORSUHAIDA BINTI AHMAD
FKTEN - NUR BAYA BINTI MOHD HASHIM
FKTEN - NUR FARAHIYAH BINTI MOHAMMAD
FKTEN - NUR HIDAYAH BINTI RAMLI
FKTEN - NUR SYAKIMAH BT ISMAIL
FKTEN - NURJULIANA BINTI JUHARI
FKTEN - NUROL HUSNA BINTI CHE ROSE
FKTEN - NURUL FADZLIN BINTI GHAZALI
FKTEN - NURUL IZZA BINTI MOHD NOR
FKTEN - NURULBARIAH BINTI IDRIS
FKTEN - NURULISMA BINTI HJ ISMAIL
FKTEN - ONG BI LYNN
FKTEN - ONG SIOK LAN
FKTEN - OUNG QI WEI
FKTEN - PRABAKARAN A/L POOPALAN
FKTEN - RASHIDAH BINTI CHE YOB
FKTEN - RAZAIDI BIN HUSSIN
FKTEN - RIZALAFANDE BIN CHE ISMAIL
FKTEN - ROHANI BINTI S MOHAMED FAROOK
FKTEN - ROSLIN BIN JAMALUDIN
FKTEN - ROZANA AINA MAULAT OSMAN
FKTEN - SABRI BIN ZAKARIA
FKTEN - SAFIZAN BINTI SHAARI
FKTEN - SAIDATUL ARDEENAWATIE BINTI AWANG
FKTEN - SANNA BINTI TAKING
FKTEN - SAUFIAH BINTI ABDUL RAHIM
FKTEN - SHAHRIR RIZAL BIN KASJOO
FKTEN - SHAIFUL NIZAM BIN MOHYAR
FKTEN - SHAKHIRUL BIN MAT SALLEH
FKTEN - SHAMSUL AMIR BIN ABDUL RAIS
FKTEN - SHAZLINA BINTI JOHARI
FKTEN - SHUHAIDA BINTI YAHUD
FKTEN - SITI AZURA BINTI ABUZAR
FKTEN - SITI JULIA BINTI ROSLI
FKTEN - SITI KHADIJAH BINTI ZA'ABA
FKTEN - SITI ZARINA BINTI MD NAZIRI
FKTEN - SOHIFUL ANUAR BIN ZAINOL MURAD
FKTEN - STEVEN A/L TANISELASS
FKTEN - SURIATI BINTI SUHAIMI
FKTEN - SYARIFAH NORFAEZAH BT SABKI
FKTEN - SYED MUHAMMAD MAMDUH BIN SYED ZAKARIA
FKTEN - THENNARASAN A/L SABAPATHY
FKTEN - TIJJANI ADAM
FKTEN - VIJAYASARVESWARI A/P VEERAPERUMAL
FKTEN - VIKNESWARAN A/L VIJEAN
FKTEN - WAN AZLIANAWATI BT WAN AZIZ
FKTEN - WAN MOKHDZANI BIN WAN NOR HAIMI
FKTEN - WEE FWEN HOON
FKTEN - YUFRIDIN BIN WAHAB
FKTEN - ZALIMAN BIN SAULI
FKTEN - ZARIMAWATY BINTI ZAILAN
FKTEN - ZULKARNAY BIN ZAKARIA
FKTEN - ZULKIFLI BIN HUSIN

FKTEN - SAIDATUL ARDEENAWATIE BINTI AWANG
FKTEN - SANNA BINTI TAKING
FKTEN - SAUFIAH BINTI ABDUL RAHIM
FKTEN - SHAHRIR RIZAL BIN KASJOO
FKTEN - SHAIFUL NIZAM BIN MOHYAR
FKTEN - SHAKHIRUL BIN MAT SALLEH
FKTEN - SHAMSUL AMIR BIN ABDUL RAIS
FKTEN - SHAZLINA BINTI JOHARI
FKTEN - SHUHAIDA BINTI YAHUD
FKTEN - SITI AZURA BINTI ABUZAR
FKTEN - SITI JULIA BINTI ROSLI
FKTEN - SITI KHADIJAH BINTI ZA'ABA
FKTEN - SITI ZARINA BINTI MD NAZIRI
FKTEN - SOHIFUL ANUAR BIN ZAINOL MURAD
FKTEN - STEVEN A/L TANISELASS
FKTEN - SURIATI BINTI SUHAIMI
FKTEN - SYARIFAH NORFAEZAH BT SABKI
FKTEN - SYED MUHAMMAD MAMDUH BIN SYED ZAKARIA
FKTEN - THENNARASAN A/L SABAPATHY
FKTEN - TIJJANI ADAM
FKTEN - VIJAYASARVESWARI A/P VEERAPERUMAL
FKTEN - VIKNESWARAN A/L VIJEAN
FKTEN - WAN AZLIANAWATI BT WAN AZIZ
FKTEN - WAN MOKHDZANI BIN WAN NOR HAIMI
FKTEN - WEE FWEN HOON
FKTEN - YUFRIDIN BIN WAHAB
FKTEN - ZALIMAN BIN SAULI
FKTEN - ZARIMAWATY BINTI ZAILAN
FKTEN - ZULKARNAY BIN ZAKARIA
FKTEN - ZULKIFLI BIN HUSIN
INEE - FOO KAI LOONG
INEE - JASNI BIN MOHAMED ISMAIL
INEE - LIU WEI WEN
INEE - MOHAMAD FARIS BIN MOHAMAD FATHIL
INEE - MOHAMMAD ISA BIN AHMAD AZAN
INEE - MOHAMMAD NUZAIHAN BIN MD NOR
INEE - NOR AZIZAH BINTI PARMIN
INEE - NUR HAMIDAH BINTI ABDUL HALIM
INEE - RAMZAN BIN MAT AYUB
INEE - RUSLINDA BINTI A. RAHIM
INEE - UDA BIN HASHIM
INEE - VOON CHUN HONG
Wednesday
Must come earlier:
FKTEN - ISZAIDY BIN ISMAIL
FKTEN - SUZANNA BINTI HARUN RONALD
Must come much earlier:
FKTEN - CHE MUHAMMAD NOR BIN CHE ISA
FKTEN - MOHAMAD SUHAIZAL BIN ABU HASSAN
FKTEN - MOHD FIKRI BIN CHE HUSIN
FKTEN - MOHD HAFIZI BIN OMAR
FKTEN - MOHD SHAIFUL AZIZ BIN RASHID ALI
FKTEN - MOHD WAFI BIN NASRUDIN
Free day:
FKTEN - ABDUL HAFIIZH BIN ISMAIL
FKTEN - AFZAN BINTI KAMARUDIN
FKTEN - AHMAD ASHRAF BIN ABDUL HALIM
FKTEN - AHMAD FARIZ BIN HASAN
FKTEN - AHMAD HUSNI BIN MOHD SHAPRI
FKTEN - AHMAD NASIR BIN CHE ROSLI
FKTEN - AHMAD NASRUL BIN NORALI
FKTEN - AHMAD SYAHIR BIN AHMAD BAKHIT
FKTEN - 'AINI SYUHADA BINTI MD ZAIN
FKTEN - AMIR NAZREN BIN ABDUL RAHIM
FKTEN - ANAS BIN MOHD NOOR
FKTEN - ANISHAZIELA BINTI AZIZAN
FKTEN - ARIF MAWARDI BIN ISMAIL @ SUHAIMEE
FKTEN - ASRAL BIN BAHARI JAMBEK
FKTEN - ASYRAF HAKIMI BIN ABU BAKAR

Border gap:
FKTEN - MOHD SHAIFUL AZIZ BIN RASHID ALI
Must stay much longer:
FKTEN - ISZAIDY BIN ISMAIL

FKTEN - WAN AZLIANAWATI BT WAN AZIZ
FKTEN - WAN MOKHDZANI BIN WAN NOR HAIMI
FKTEN - WEE FWEN HOON
FKTEN - YUFRIDIN BIN WAHAB
FKTEN - ZALIMAN BIN SAULI
FKTEN - ZARIMAWATY BINTI ZAILAN
FKTEN - ZULKARNAY BIN ZAKARIA
FKTEN - ZULKIFLI BIN HUSIN
INEE - FOO KAI LOONG
INEE - JASNI BIN MOHAMED ISMAIL
INEE - LIU WEI WEN
INEE - MOHAMAD FARIS BIN MOHAMAD FATHIL
INEE - MOHAMMAD ISA BIN AHMAD AZAN
INEE - MOHAMMAD NUZAIHAN BIN MD NOR
INEE - NOR AZIZAH BINTI PARMIN
INEE - NUR HAMIDAH BINTI ABDUL HALIM
INEE - RAMZAN BIN MAT AYUB
INEE - RUSLINDA BINTI A. RAHIM
INEE - UDA BIN HASHIM
INEE - VOON CHUN HONG
Must stay much longer:
FKTEN - HASNAH BINTI AHMAD
FKTEN - MOHAMAD SUHAIZAL BIN ABU HASSAN
Free day:
FKTEN - ABDUL HAFIIZH BIN ISMAIL
FKTEN - AFZAN BINTI KAMARUDIN
FKTEN - AHMAD ASHRAF BIN ABDUL HALIM
FKTEN - AHMAD FARIZ BIN HASAN
FKTEN - AHMAD HUSNI BIN MOHD SHAPRI
FKTEN - AHMAD NASRUL BIN NORALI
FKTEN - AHMAD SYAHIR BIN AHMAD BAKHIT
FKTEN - 'AINI SYUHADA BINTI MD ZAIN
FKTEN - AMIR NAZREN BIN ABDUL RAHIM
FKTEN - ANAS BIN MOHD NOOR
FKTEN - ANISHAZIELA BINTI AZIZAN
FKTEN - ARIF MAWARDI BIN ISMAIL @ SUHAIMEE
FKTEN - ASRAL BIN BAHARI JAMBEK
FKTEN - ASYRAF HAKIMI BIN ABU BAKAR
FKTEN - AZIAN AZAMIMI BINTI ABDULLAH
FKTEN - AZIZI BIN HARUN
FKTEN - AZNOR HANAH BINTI ABDUL HALIM
FKTEN - BANU A/P POOBALAN
FKTEN - BIBI NADIA BINTI TAIB
FKTEN - CHE MUHAMMAD NOR BIN CHE ISA
FKTEN - CHE WAN SHARIFAH ROBIAH BINTI MOHAMAD
FKTEN - CHENG EE MENG
FKTEN - EMI IZHANIZAM BIN AZMI
FKTEN - FAIRUL AFZAL BIN AHMAD FUAD
FKTEN - FAIZAH BINTI ABU BAKAR
FKTEN - FARADILLA BINTI AZIZ
FKTEN - FARIDAH BINTI HASSAN
FKTEN - HAMZAH BIN SAKERAN
FKTEN - HANIZA BINTI YAZID
FKTEN - HARIYANTI BINTI MOHD SALEH
FKTEN - HASLIZA BINTI A RAHIM @ SAMSUDDIN
FKTEN - HASNIZAH BT ARIS
FKTEN - HUSNA BINTI JAMAL ABDUL NASIR
FKTEN - ILI SALWANI BINTI MOHAMAD
FKTEN - ISMAHAYATI BINTI ADAM
FKTEN - ISZAIDY BIN ISMAIL
FKTEN - KHAIRUL NAJMY BIN ABDUL RANI
FKTEN - LEE HOI LEONG
FKTEN - LIM CHEE CHIN
FKTEN - LIYANA BINTI ZAHID
FKTEN - MAZLEE BIN MAZALAN
FKTEN - MD MAMUN BIN IBNE REAZ
FKTEN - MOHAMAD HALIM BIN ABD. WAHID
FKTEN - MOHAMAD RIZAL BIN ABDUL REJAB
FKTEN - MOHAMED ELSHAIKH ELOBAID SAID AHMED
FKTEN - MOHAMED NASRUN BIN OSMAN
FKTEN - MOHAMMAD SHAHRIL BIN SALIM
FKTEN - MOHD AMINUDIN BIN JAMLOS
FKTEN - MOHD FAIRUS BIN AHMAD
FKTEN - MOHD FAIRUZ BIN MUHAMAD FADZIL
FKTEN - MOHD FHAIZAL BIN RADZI
FKTEN - MOHD FIKRI BIN CHE HUSIN
FKTEN - MOHD HAFIZ BIN ISMAIL
FKTEN - MOHD HAFIZI BIN OMAR
FKTEN - MOHD HAFIZUDDIN BIN MAT
FKTEN - MOHD HANAFI BIN MAT SOM
FKTEN - MOHD KHAIRUDDIN BIN MD ARSHAD
FKTEN - MOHD KHAIRUL BIN MD KAMIL
FKTEN - MOHD NAJIB BIN MOHD YASIN
FKTEN - MOHD NATASHAH BIN NORIZAN
FKTEN - MOHD NAZRI BIN A. KARIM
FKTEN - MOHD NAZRIN BIN MD ISA
FKTEN - MOHD NORHAFIZ BIN HASHIM
FKTEN - MOHD ROSYDI BIN ZAKARIA
FKTEN - MOHD SALLEHUDIN BIN SAAD
FKTEN - MOHD SYAHRIL BIN NOOR SHAH
FKTEN - MOHD WAFI BIN NASRUDIN
FKTEN - MOHD YUSOFF BIN MASHOR
FKTEN - MOHD ZAMRI BIN ZAHIR AHMAD
FKTEN - MOHD. AZARULSANI BIN MD. AZIDIN
FKTEN - MUAMMAR BIN MOHAMAD ISA
FKTEN - MUHAMAD ASMI BIN ROMLI
FKTEN - MUHAMAD HAFIZ BIN AB AZIZ
FKTEN - MUHAMAD SANI BIN MUSTAFA
FKTEN - MUHAMMAD MAHYIDDIN BIN RAMLI
FKTEN - MUHAMMAD SOLIHIN BIN ZULKEFLI
FKTEN - MUHD NUR RAHMAN BIN YAHYA
FKTEN - MUZAMMIL BIN JUSOH
FKTEN - NAIMAH BINTI YAAKOB
FKTEN - NASHRUL FAZLI BIN MOHD NASIR
FKTEN - NAZAHAH BINTI MUSTAFA
FKTEN - NAZUHUSNA BINTI KHALID
FKTEN - NOOR ALIA BINTI MD ZAIN
FKTEN - NOOR SYAZANA BINTI ARSHAD
FKTEN - NOR AZURA MALINI BINTI AHMAD HAMBALI
FKTEN - NOR FARHANI BINTI ZAKARIA
FKTEN - NOR HIDAYAH BINTI SAAD
FKTEN - NOR ROSHIDAH BINTI YUSOF
FKTEN - NORAINI BINTI OTHMAN
FKTEN - NORAZEANI BINTI ABDUL RAHMAN
FKTEN - NORDIANA BT MOHAMAD SAAID
FKTEN - NORFATIHAH BINTI BAHARI
FKTEN - NORFAZLIN BINTI RASHID
FKTEN - NORHAWATI BINTI AHMAD
FKTEN - NORHAYATI BINTI SABANI
FKTEN - NORIZAN BINTI MOHAMED NAWAWI
FKTEN - NORLIDA BT ABU BAKAR
FKTEN - NORMAHIRA BINTI MAMAT @ MOHAMAD NOR
FKTEN - NORMALIZA BINTI OMAR
FKTEN - NORSHAMSURI BIN ALI @ HASIM
FKTEN - NORSUHAIDA BINTI AHMAD
FKTEN - NUR BAYA BINTI MOHD HASHIM
FKTEN - NUR FARAHIYAH BINTI MOHAMMAD
FKTEN - NUR HIDAYAH BINTI RAMLI
FKTEN - NUR SYAKIMAH BT ISMAIL
FKTEN - NURJULIANA BINTI JUHARI
FKTEN - NUROL HUSNA BINTI CHE ROSE
FKTEN - NURUL FADZLIN BINTI GHAZALI
FKTEN - NURUL IZZA BINTI MOHD NOR
FKTEN - NURULBARIAH BINTI IDRIS
FKTEN - NURULISMA BINTI HJ ISMAIL
FKTEN - ONG BI LYNN
FKTEN - ONG SIOK LAN
FKTEN - OUNG QI WEI
FKTEN - PRABAKARAN A/L POOPALAN
FKTEN - RASHIDAH BINTI CHE YOB
FKTEN - RAZAIDI BIN HUSSIN
FKTEN - RIZALAFANDE BIN CHE ISMAIL
FKTEN - ROHANI BINTI S MOHAMED FAROOK
FKTEN - ROSLIN BIN JAMALUDIN
FKTEN - ROZANA AINA MAULAT OSMAN
FKTEN - SABRI BIN ZAKARIA
FKTEN - SAFIZAN BINTI SHAARI
FKTEN - SAIDATUL ARDEENAWATIE BINTI AWANG
FKTEN - SANNA BINTI TAKING
FKTEN - SAUFIAH BINTI ABDUL RAHIM
FKTEN - SHAHRIR RIZAL BIN KASJOO
FKTEN - SHAIFUL NIZAM BIN MOHYAR
FKTEN - SHAKHIRUL BIN MAT SALLEH
FKTEN - SHAMSUL AMIR BIN ABDUL RAIS
FKTEN - SHAZLINA BINTI JOHARI
FKTEN - SHUHAIDA BINTI YAHUD
FKTEN - SITI AZURA BINTI ABUZAR
FKTEN - SITI JULIA BINTI ROSLI
FKTEN - SITI KHADIJAH BINTI ZA'ABA
FKTEN - SITI ZARINA BINTI MD NAZIRI
FKTEN - SOHIFUL ANUAR BIN ZAINOL MURAD
FKTEN - STEVEN A/L TANISELASS
FKTEN - SURIATI BINTI SUHAIMI
FKTEN - SYARIFAH NORFAEZAH BT SABKI
FKTEN - SYED MUHAMMAD MAMDUH BIN SYED ZAKARIA

FKTEN - SAIDATUL ARDEENAWATIE BINTI AWANG
FKTEN - SANNA BINTI TAKING
FKTEN - SAUFIAH BINTI ABDUL RAHIM
FKTEN - SHAHRIR RIZAL BIN KASJOO
FKTEN - SHAIFUL NIZAM BIN MOHYAR
FKTEN - SHAKHIRUL BIN MAT SALLEH
FKTEN - SHAMSUL AMIR BIN ABDUL RAIS
FKTEN - SHAZLINA BINTI JOHARI
FKTEN - SHUHAIDA BINTI YAHUD
FKTEN - SITI AZURA BINTI ABUZAR
FKTEN - SITI JULIA BINTI ROSLI
FKTEN - SITI KHADIJAH BINTI ZA'ABA
FKTEN - SITI ZARINA BINTI MD NAZIRI
FKTEN - SOHIFUL ANUAR BIN ZAINOL MURAD
FKTEN - STEVEN A/L TANISELASS
FKTEN - SURIATI BINTI SUHAIMI
FKTEN - SYARIFAH NORFAEZAH BT SABKI
FKTEN - SYED MUHAMMAD MAMDUH BIN SYED ZAKARIA
FKTEN - THENNARASAN A/L SABAPATHY
FKTEN - TIJJANI ADAM
FKTEN - VIJAYASARVESWARI A/P VEERAPERUMAL
FKTEN - VIKNESWARAN A/L VIJEAN
FKTEN - WAN AZLIANAWATI BT WAN AZIZ
FKTEN - WAN MOKHDZANI BIN WAN NOR HAIMI
FKTEN - WEE FWEN HOON
FKTEN - YUFRIDIN BIN WAHAB
FKTEN - ZALIMAN BIN SAULI
FKTEN - ZARIMAWATY BINTI ZAILAN
FKTEN - ZULKARNAY BIN ZAKARIA
FKTEN - ZULKIFLI BIN HUSIN
INEE - FOO KAI LOONG
INEE - JASNI BIN MOHAMED ISMAIL
INEE - LIU WEI WEN
INEE - MOHAMAD FARIS BIN MOHAMAD FATHIL
INEE - MOHAMMAD ISA BIN AHMAD AZAN
INEE - MOHAMMAD NUZAIHAN BIN MD NOR
INEE - NOR AZIZAH BINTI PARMIN
INEE - NUR HAMIDAH BINTI ABDUL HALIM
INEE - RAMZAN BIN MAT AYUB
INEE - RUSLINDA BINTI A. RAHIM
INEE - UDA BIN HASHIM
INEE - VOON CHUN HONG
Must stay longer:
FKTEN - HAZILA BINTI OTHMAN
Must stay much longer:
FKTEN - AHMAD NASIR BIN CHE ROSLI
FKTEN - HASNAH BINTI AHMAD
FKTEN - MOHD SHAIFUL AZIZ BIN RASHID ALI
FKTEN - MOHD WAFI BIN NASRUDIN
FKTEN - SUZANNA BINTI HARUN RONALD
Free day:
FKTEN - ABDUL HAFIIZH BIN ISMAIL
FKTEN - AFZAN BINTI KAMARUDIN
FKTEN - AHMAD ASHRAF BIN ABDUL HALIM
FKTEN - AHMAD FARIZ BIN HASAN
FKTEN - AHMAD HUSNI BIN MOHD SHAPRI
FKTEN - AHMAD NASRUL BIN NORALI
FKTEN - AHMAD SYAHIR BIN AHMAD BAKHIT
FKTEN - 'AINI SYUHADA BINTI MD ZAIN
FKTEN - AMIR NAZREN BIN ABDUL RAHIM
FKTEN - ANAS BIN MOHD NOOR
FKTEN - ANISHAZIELA BINTI AZIZAN
FKTEN - ARIF MAWARDI BIN ISMAIL @ SUHAIMEE
FKTEN - ASRAL BIN BAHARI JAMBEK
FKTEN - ASYRAF HAKIMI BIN ABU BAKAR
FKTEN - AZIAN AZAMIMI BINTI ABDULLAH
FKTEN - AZIZI BIN HARUN
FKTEN - AZNOR HANAH BINTI ABDUL HALIM
FKTEN - BANU A/P POOBALAN
FKTEN - BIBI NADIA BINTI TAIB
FKTEN - CHE WAN SHARIFAH ROBIAH BINTI MOHAMAD
FKTEN - CHENG EE MENG
FKTEN - EMI IZHANIZAM BIN AZMI
FKTEN - FAIRUL AFZAL BIN AHMAD FUAD
FKTEN - FAIZAH BINTI ABU BAKAR
FKTEN - FARADILLA BINTI AZIZ
FKTEN - FARIDAH BINTI HASSAN
FKTEN - HAMZAH BIN SAKERAN
FKTEN - HANIZA BINTI YAZID
FKTEN - HARIYANTI BINTI MOHD SALEH
FKTEN - HASLIZA BINTI A RAHIM @ SAMSUDDIN
FKTEN - HASNIZAH BT ARIS
FKTEN - HUSNA BINTI JAMAL ABDUL NASIR
FKTEN - ILI SALWANI BINTI MOHAMAD
FKTEN - ISMAHAYATI BINTI ADAM
FKTEN - ISZAIDY BIN ISMAIL
FKTEN - KHAIRUL NAJMY BIN ABDUL RANI
FKTEN - LEE HOI LEONG
FKTEN - LIM CHEE CHIN
FKTEN - LIYANA BINTI ZAHID
FKTEN - MAZLEE BIN MAZALAN
FKTEN - MD MAMUN BIN IBNE REAZ
FKTEN - MOHAMAD HALIM BIN ABD. WAHID
FKTEN - MOHAMAD RIZAL BIN ABDUL REJAB
FKTEN - MOHAMED ELSHAIKH ELOBAID SAID AHMED
FKTEN - MOHAMED NASRUN BIN OSMAN
FKTEN - MOHAMMAD SHAHRIL BIN SALIM
FKTEN - MOHD AMINUDIN BIN JAMLOS
FKTEN - MOHD FAIRUS BIN AHMAD
FKTEN - MOHD FAIRUZ BIN MUHAMAD FADZIL
FKTEN - MOHD FHAIZAL BIN RADZI
FKTEN - MOHD FIKRI BIN CHE HUSIN
FKTEN - MOHD HAFIZ BIN ISMAIL
FKTEN - MOHD HAFIZUDDIN BIN MAT
FKTEN - MOHD HANAFI BIN MAT SOM
FKTEN - MOHD KHAIRUDDIN BIN MD ARSHAD
FKTEN - MOHD KHAIRUL BIN MD KAMIL
FKTEN - MOHD NAJIB BIN MOHD YASIN
FKTEN - MOHD NATASHAH BIN NORIZAN
FKTEN - MOHD NAZRI BIN A. KARIM
FKTEN - MOHD NAZRIN BIN MD ISA
FKTEN - MOHD NORHAFIZ BIN HASHIM
FKTEN - MOHD ROSYDI BIN ZAKARIA
FKTEN - MOHD SALLEHUDIN BIN SAAD
FKTEN - MOHD SYAHRIL BIN NOOR SHAH
FKTEN - MOHD YUSOFF BIN MASHOR
FKTEN - MOHD ZAMRI BIN ZAHIR AHMAD
FKTEN - MOHD. AZARULSANI BIN MD. AZIDIN
FKTEN - MUAMMAR BIN MOHAMAD ISA
FKTEN - MUHAMAD ASMI BIN ROMLI
FKTEN - MUHAMAD HAFIZ BIN AB AZIZ
FKTEN - MUHAMAD SANI BIN MUSTAFA
FKTEN - MUHAMMAD MAHYIDDIN BIN RAMLI
FKTEN - MUHAMMAD SOLIHIN BIN ZULKEFLI
FKTEN - MUHD NUR RAHMAN BIN YAHYA
FKTEN - MUZAMMIL BIN JUSOH
FKTEN - NAIMAH BINTI YAAKOB
FKTEN - NASHRUL FAZLI BIN MOHD NASIR
FKTEN - NAZAHAH BINTI MUSTAFA
FKTEN - NAZUHUSNA BINTI KHALID
FKTEN - NOOR ALIA BINTI MD ZAIN
FKTEN - NOOR SYAZANA BINTI ARSHAD
FKTEN - NOR AZURA MALINI BINTI AHMAD HAMBALI
FKTEN - NOR FARHANI BINTI ZAKARIA
FKTEN - NOR HIDAYAH BINTI SAAD
FKTEN - NOR ROSHIDAH BINTI YUSOF
FKTEN - NORAINI BINTI OTHMAN
FKTEN - NORAZEANI BINTI ABDUL RAHMAN
FKTEN - NORDIANA BT MOHAMAD SAAID
FKTEN - NORFATIHAH BINTI BAHARI
FKTEN - NORFAZLIN BINTI RASHID
FKTEN - NORHAWATI BINTI AHMAD
FKTEN - NORHAYATI BINTI SABANI
FKTEN - NORIZAN BINTI MOHAMED NAWAWI
FKTEN - NORLIDA BT ABU BAKAR
FKTEN - NORMAHIRA BINTI MAMAT @ MOHAMAD NOR
FKTEN - NORMALIZA BINTI OMAR
FKTEN - NORSHAMSURI BIN ALI @ HASIM
FKTEN - NORSUHAIDA BINTI AHMAD
FKTEN - NUR BAYA BINTI MOHD HASHIM
FKTEN - NUR FARAHIYAH BINTI MOHAMMAD
FKTEN - NUR HIDAYAH BINTI RAMLI
FKTEN - NUR SYAKIMAH BT ISMAIL
FKTEN - NURJULIANA BINTI JUHARI
